- [>] [P0] `AIW-174` **CONDITIONAL DERIVATION ACHIEVED (S293 2026-08-07) — reduces to ONE premise, and the paper already holds it. Note: `drafts/aiw174-tsirelson-from-capacity.md`** — *S293: the derivation does not run the way the item assumed. Deriving IC from a capacity bound is the wrong shape; the two are **the same inequality** — IC's Σ_k I(a_k:b|K=k) ≤ m is the holographic bound on the shared locus, once m is read as the locus's capacity rather than a transmitted-bit count. So nothing needs proving between two principles; what needs justifying is one **single-locus decoding postulate** (the pair's joint statistics are decodings of one boundary locus, whose jointly extractable information is capacity-bounded). Granted it, the Pawłowski/Uffink chain gives CHSH ≤ 2√2 with no new machinery. **And that postulate is §5.2's single-surface ontology**, which the paper adopts to unify singularities — for reasons with nothing to do with Bell. An ontological commitment made elsewhere turns out to carry a quantitative correlation bound. **The Oughton & Timpson objection is ANSWERED** (their Entropy 26(7) 562 = correct; IC's Tsirelson recovery is Shannon-specific): the measure is not chosen at the principle level, it is inherited — the rigorous Bekenstein bounds are α=1 Umegaki relative-entropy statements (Casini 2008; Longo arXiv:2409.14408; **Kudler-Flam et al. PRD 111, 105001 — handover citation CONFIRMED correct by DOI**, do not "fix" it to the similar 025013 paper), quantum mutual information *is* a relative entropy, and on the protocol's classical registers the von Neumann quantity reduces to Shannon by restriction, not stipulation. Honest limit recorded: a Rényi-α analogue of the 2πER inequality would weaken the reply. **Success criterion NOT met** — this is conditional, not "from capacity alone", and it is not a no-go either. Also recorded so it is not re-attempted: the communication-complexity route (van Dam; Brassard et al. PRL 96, 250401, threshold ≈3.266) **fails at the same joint** — a capacity bound limits the density of nonlocal resources per unit area, not the strength of one locus. Proposed §6.5 replacement text is drafted in the note's §9 and **NOT applied — awaiting MG**.* — *(S291 2026-08-07, Fable cosmology sweep; **MG-set P0**: "yes P0 next session together with 172")*: The S291 literature sweep confirmed that **no work connects information causality or Tsirelson's bound to holography or the Bekenstein bound as of mid-2026** — §6.5 of SB-HC4A already flags this route as its stated open obligation, so the paper has publicly staked the direction without anyone else working it. This is the paper's best novelty claim. Concrete first step: model the boundary as a finite-capacity channel (capacity fixed by the area bound), impose that any interior-pair decoding is a decoding of ONE boundary locus, and push the Jain, Gachechiladze & Miklin polynomial-inequality machinery (PRL 133, 160201, arXiv:2308.02478; extended arXiv:2606.02416) through it. **Confront the Oughton & Timpson objection head-on** (Entropy 26(7) 562 — IC's recovery of Tsirelson is Shannon-specific and fails under Rényi-2): the principled answer is that the rigorous Bekenstein bounds are **von Neumann relative-entropy** statements (Kudler-Flam et al., PRD 111, 105001; Longo arXiv:2409.14408). SUCCESS = CHSH ≤ 2√2 from capacity assumptions alone. INSTRUCTIVE FAILURE = only Uffink-level bounds, which is itself publishable as a no-go. ⚠ Interacts with `AIW-172` error 2 — the Bekenstein bound is currently misattributed as area-proportional throughout the paper, and this derivation needs the bound stated correctly, so do the `AIW-172` repair first or in parallel.

FRAME→SELF pre-registered as `B = M @ [I│−I]`, `M ≥ 0`; L1 oracle floor **0.0097** with the frame carried at corr **+0.966**, which confirms **τ = 0.1**; scoring protocol = θ equilibrated over the stimulus **ensemble** then **PINNED**, T = 6400, 32 stimuli, independent noise streams for target vs arm; structural criticality **ρ = 0.269 by spectral radius** (NOT `branching_sigma` at rest, degenerate for this rig). Still genuinely absent: Jstab and any L2/L3 spiking numbers. — **(D) METHOD-SECTION LESSONS** (all crucible-earned, all transferable): (i) an oracle's hand-installed lateral strength σ does **not** transfer to a plastic learner (laterals are emergent, only on ~5% pre-existing edges — plasticity cannot create edges); (ii) within/cross on TOTAL weight is baseline-confounded → read build-specificity as **Δw vs a frozen baseline**; (iii) **a tight spread across a few seeds is evidence of a tight effect ONLY if the seed axis varies every source of run-to-run variation** — one `seed` argument set both wiring and noise stream, so three draws moved in lockstep and looked like precision (sd 0.0058) against a true noise sd of 0.087; (iv) crucible's measurement-discipline patterns (its `docs/didactic-patterns.md` #14–26) are the reusable set — read them when writing methods, they are deliberately not in aIware's didactic registry. **Crucible owns the experimental record; aIware owns the paper and every claim in it.** — **(E) THE OPERATIONALIZATION CORRECTION, MG-directed 2026-07-31 — this one reframes the whole method, and it was one of the 30 inbox items hidden by the case-sensitivity bug (see `AIW-143`), so it had never reached an aIware session before S284.** **The behavioral-task approach to demonstrating closure is a CATEGORY ERROR and nulled six times** — the companion's closure claims must not rest on an ON/OFF task-score difference. Crucible designed task after task where toggling `closure_enabled` should move a behavioral DV (hidden-norm reversal; DXM XOR; DSH delay-hold + a Π-onto-CE/curriculum rescue) and **every one came back closure ON ≈ OFF** (never beating OFF by >~0.05 across 20+ runs, plus the older CRU-40 leaky null). **Root cause (MG): closure is not a performance-enhancer bolted onto a task-learner — closure IS the free-modeling.** The consciousness-relevant closure is the **ESM↔EWM free-modeling loop with the ESM identified as the entity**: `EWM→ESM` always live, **`ESM→EWM` is the knob**, and OFF means the self observes but cannot re-enter — a spectator. **Free modeling = play = consciousness, one phenomenon:** play is spontaneous, reward-free, body-optional free-modeling (imitation/rehearsal) and it is the **construction phase that BUILDS the implicit models**; afterwards goal-directed exploitation runs straight at satisfaction and play recedes. The node-perturbation learning-rule "wall" crucible hit (learns only a linearly-separable 1-bit map) **IS the closure-OFF condition** — an entity that cannot play, trying to be intelligent directly. **Method consequence for the paper:** frame the closure demonstration as a **reward-free two-phase play→goal** experiment (phase 1: closure ON free-models/rehearses-as-self, self-supervised Hebbian consolidation into W; phase 2: can it USE the built model — ON yes, OFF no), with **state-wipe transfer as the DV** — never a rewarded ON/OFF task score. Note this is the *same* insight as (B)'s consolidation-channel reframe arriving from a different direction, and it is consistent with the later redeployment GO. Source: crucible `docs/design/cru57-closure-as-free-modeling-play.md` + `docs/decisions.md`.

- [ ] [P2] `AIW-187` **THE ALLOCATION QUESTION — what does a saturated boundary fail to encode first?** *(S297 2026-08-08, spun out of `AIW-186`; **outranks `AIW-166` §8 item 1, the extremal/minimal seam**)*: The saturation account of the smooth initial condition needs a **maximally disordered** boundary (§5.4's own words) to decompress into a **low gravitational entropy** interior. That is Penrose's Weyl-curvature asymmetry stated precisely: at the bang, matter degrees of freedom are **thermalized** (the CMB is the most perfect blackbody ever measured) while gravitational ones are **unexcited** — maxed and minimal at the same instant. Penrose **stipulates** vanishing Weyl curvature; CCC inherits the question rather than dissolving it. **An automaton has a resource he does not: the boundary is a *capacity*, so "which degrees of freedom get written" is an allocation question, not a stipulation.** **Candidate answer (`drafts/aiw186-vacuity-regime.md` §7a):** the asymmetry is only paradoxical in thermodynamic units — in **description-length** units, thermal equilibrium is the *cheapest macrostate there is* (one number for T, one for density), while clumped structure is expensive (you must say *where* everything is). So a capacity-bounded decompression writes the cheapest specifiable interior, which is smooth and thermal — the observed initial condition — and **Weyl curvature tracks how much of the boundary's content has been *expressed* as interior structure**: zero at the bang, growing monotonically as the orbit runs, maximal at the clumped end-state where §5.4's instability triggers. Nothing has to *move*; the boundary encodes the total throughout. This is `aiw166-cosmos-transfer.md` §3's generator/output distinction doing real work rather than serving as an analogy, and it is why inflation cannot reach this explanandum — inflation operates **inside the orbit** and cannot speak about the seed. **What it does NOT give: numbers.** Route to numbers, unattempted: *(a) amplitude* — a finite-capacity boundary cannot specify a *perfectly* uniform interior (exact uniformity is infinite-precision), so the residual inhomogeneity is a **forced consequence of the capacity bound**, which is the span law's near-ceiling condition arriving from the encoding side; *(b) tilt* — `n_s ≈ 0.965` needs the **allocation rule across scales** (constant amplitude per octave = equal capacity per scale octave; red tilt = slightly more capacity at large scales); *(c) the gate on both* — **one rule must give amplitude AND tilt, or it is fitting.** ⚠ Numerology trap flagged in `aiw166` §6a: the ~10⁻⁵ ↔ ~10⁴–10⁵-patches coincidence invites a residual-counting argument whose naive form is white noise, `P(k) ∝ k⁴`. **Prize if it lands:** Penrose's Weyl-curvature hypothesis as a *consequence* rather than a stipulation — larger than the smoothness it was chasing. **▸ S297 2026-08-08 — WORKED, and it produced a number: `drafts/aiw187-allocation.md`.** **(a) An unexpected asset — the SIGN of the tilt comes out right from architecture, not from fitting.** Boundary capacity is area-metered and the horizon **grows**, so larger scales were specified when less capacity existed, hence at coarser precision, hence with larger amplitude: **more power at large scales is a red tilt, and `n_s < 1` follows from capacity *accreting*** with nothing chosen to produce it. Exact scale-invariance is the constant-capacity limit. **(b) ⚠ THE FIRST CONCRETE VERSION IS EXCLUDED.** A tilt sourced by the growth of a single geometric quantity, with no potential-curvature analogue, **locks `n_s` to `r`**: `n_s − 1 = −6ε_V` with `n_s = 0.965` gives `ε_V ≈ 0.0058` and **`r ≈ 0.09`**, against the observed **`r < 0.036`** (BICEP/Keck, 95% CL) — excluded by ~3×, and the tighter `n_s − 1 = −4ε` variant gives `r ≈ 0.14`, worse. **The account made concrete in the obvious way reproduces the excluded `m²φ²` corner** (`n_s ≈ 0.967`, `r ≈ 0.13`). **⇒ The requirement on any successful version is now stated and is not vague: it must BREAK the `n_s`–`r` link**, via a second parameter governing how capacity is *allocated across scales* independently of how fast capacity *grows* — an η-analogue that is not a reintroduced free potential. **(c) Provisional, not decisive:** the account does not yet derive `δ` from `C`, so the scaling exponent is stipulated and the slow-roll mapping imports machinery the account may not be subject to. **(d) ⚠ AND A STRUCTURAL PROBLEM THAT OUTRANKS THE NUMBER: "specified at horizon crossing" is INFLATION'S apparatus.** If the account needs a quasi-de-Sitter background to state itself it is **not a rival to inflation but a redescription of it**, and forfeits the horizon-problem rivalry. The bounce framing avoids this and is where the account belongs on independent grounds — but then **scale allocation must be stated without horizon crossing**, i.e. how the saturated boundary's bits map onto comoving scales of the successor interior. **⇒ NEXT STEP IS NOT A BETTER ESTIMATE — it is to state the allocation rule in bounce language.** Single blocking item, zero GPU, no new physics; (b)'s number only becomes meaningful once it exists. **(e) Unifying diagnosis worth stating once rather than rediscovering per attempt:** the residual-counting route fails on spectral shape (`P(k) ∝ k⁴`) and this one fails on the tensor bound, and both are the same fact — **a one-parameter account cannot independently fit amplitude and tilt.**

In a theory with a hard capacity bound that is a physical difference, and it **bites hardest when the horizon is small**: at early times `K(rule)` is an O(1) fraction of the budget, so a closed universe is capacity-starved in a way an exogenous-rule universe is not.
